sub Scd_MOV_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
dim iPV,oShape,index
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iPV=oSource.value
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV="Bad"
    else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
	oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
	oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
	oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & index
	iOP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_op")
    index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOP" & iOP) 
    oShape.objects("PlgC").styleclass = "PlgC_" & index
	oShape.objects("PlgO").styleclass = "PlgO_" & index
	if cstr(iPV)="Bad" then 
		iBad="B" 
	elseif iPV=3 or iPV=4 or iPV=5 or iPV=7 then 
		iBad="B"
	end if   
	blf=false
	if iBad="B" then
		oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
	else 
		if oShape.getCustomProperty("Point","tagnameLF")<>"" then
			if oSource.datavalue("tagnameLF.cp_pvLF")=cint(oshape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_LFActive")) then
				bLF=true
				if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC"))<>"Nothing" then 
					oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass="WdgC_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV3")	   
				end if
			end if   
		end if	 
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_MOV_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description

end sub


sub Scd_MOV_FG_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
dim iPV,oShape,index
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iPV=oSource.value
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV="Bad"
    else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
    oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
    oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
	oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_MOV"	
	if iPV="Bad" then
       oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & index
       oShape.objects("PlgC").style.visibility = "hidden"
	   oShape.objects("PlgO").style.visibility = "hidden"
    else
	   if oShape.getcustomProperty("Point","TagnameTrq")<>"" then
			iTorque=oSource.datavalue("TagnameTrq.cp_pv")
			sStyleTrq=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForTorque" & iTorque)
			oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & sStyleTrq 
   	   else	
	        oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_MOV"
       end if
	   
	   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then
   		   iOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
		   indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOP" & iOP) 
           oShape.objects("PlgO").styleclass="PlgO_" & indexOP
		   oShape.objects("PlgC").styleclass="PlgC_" & indexOP
	   end if
    end if	
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_MOV_FG_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'----------------------------------------
'Fire and Gas
'----------------------------------------

Sub Scd_HornBeacon_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape,iQuality(2)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iQuality(0)=oSource.quality
	iQuality(1)=oSource.dataquality("TagnameB.cp_pv")
	for j=0 to 1
		ipQuality = 4
		ipQuality = iQuality(j)
		if (ipQuality and 8)=8 then
      		sOffScan="Active"
			elseif (ipQuality and 4)=4 then
      		sBad="Bad"
    	end if 
	next
	if sOffScan="Active" then
	  	oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif sBad="Bad"then
	    oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
  		oShape.objects("DetBody").styleclass="DetBody_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad") 
		oShape.objects("WdgBeac").styleclass="WdgBeac_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")  		
	else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		iPV=oSource.value
		iPVbc=oSource.datavalue("TagnameB.cp_pv")
		index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
		indexbc=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPVbc)
		oShape.objects("DetBody").styleclass="DetBody_" & index
		oShape.objects("WdgBeac").styleclass="WdgBeac_" & indexbc
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_HornBeacon_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description       
end sub


sub FG_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape,iQuality(2)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if oShape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_orientation")<>"" then oShape.objects("WdgB").rotation=oShape.getCustomProperty("Num","cp_orientation")
	set DetBody=oShape.objects("DetBody")
	iQuality(0)=oSource.quality
	iQuality(1)=oSource.dataquality("TagnameInh.cp_inh_pv")
	for j=0 to 1
		ipQuality = 4
		ipQuality = iQuality(j)
		if (ipQuality and 8)=8 then
      		sOffScan="Active"
			elseif (ipQuality and 4)=4 then
      		sBad="Bad"
    	end if 
	next
	if sOffScan="Active" then
	  	oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
		DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForInhPV")
	elseif sBad="Bad"then
	    oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
  		DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPVBad")  		
	else
  		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
		index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
		DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & index
		if oSource.datavalue("TagnameInh.cp_inh_op")=1 then 
			index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForInhOP")
			DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & index
		end if
		if oSource.datavalue("TagnameInh.cp_inh_pv")=1 then
			index=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForInhPV")
         	DetBody.styleclass="DetBody_" & index
        end if
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " FG_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub


'*******************************************************************************************
'Channels and RTUs
'*******************************************************************************************
sub Scd_ChnRtu_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next
    set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if oSOurce.value=0 then
		oShape.objects("IndbarB").style.visibility="hidden"
		oShape.objects("OvlStsB").style.visibility="hidden"
		oShape.objects("TxtLinkB").style.visibility="hidden"
		oShape.objects("LinScaleB").style.visibility="hidden"
		oShape.objects("TxtLinkA").value="  Lnk"
	else
		oShape.objects("IndbarB").style.visibility="inherit"
		oShape.objects("OvlStsB").style.visibility="inherit"
		oShape.objects("TxtLinkB").style.visibility="inherit"
		oShape.objects("LinScaleB").style.visibility="inherit"
		oShape.objects("TxtLinkA").value="Lnk A"
	end if	
    if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_BarFail") then 
	    oShape.objects("IndbarA").rangeHi=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_BarFail")
	    oShape.objects("IndbarB").rangeHi=oShape.objects("IndbarA").rangeHi
	    oShape.objects("IndbarA").rangeLo=0
	    oShape.objects("IndbarB").rangeLo=0
	end if   	
    if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_LnkABarCnt") then
		oShape.objects("IndbarA").value=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_LnkABarCnt")
	end if
	if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_LnkBBarCnt") then
		oShape.objects("IndbarB").value=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_LnkBBarCnt")
	end if
	if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_LnkStsA") then
		oShape.objects("OvlStsA").styleclass="OvlStsA_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForSts" & oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_LnkStsA") )
		oShape.objects("IndbarA").styleclass="IndbarA_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForSts" & oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_LnkStsA") )
	end if
	if oSource.DataChanged("tagname.cp_LnkStsB") then
		oShape.objects("OvlStsB").styleclass="OvlStsB_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForSts" & oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_LnkStsB") )
		oShape.objects("IndbarB").styleclass="IndbarB_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForSts" & oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_LnkStsB") )
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_ChnRtu_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'*******************************************************************************************
'MOS Key
'*******************************************************************************************

sub Scd_MntOvrKey_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next
    set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
    iPV=oSource.value
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)=iQualityOffScan then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	elseif (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	else
		oShape.objects("LinExecState").styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	end if
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
    oShape.objects("GrpKey").styleclass=index
end sub</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript event=onupdate for=shape001_AlpTrendGrp defer>on error resume next
	if bTrendset = false then
		TrendNo = window.external.Dictionary.Item("TrendNo")
		if err.number<>0 then
			window.external.Dictionary.Add "TrendNo", 1
			me.value=1
		else
			me.value = TrendNo
		end if
		bTrendset = true
	end if
	window.external.Dictionary.Item("TrendNo").value=me.value
	window.settimeout "is_empty", 200
	if bLoaded=false then
   		window.settimeout "upd_chart", 1000
   		bloaded=true
	end if
	if document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("TrdSel"))<>"" then
   		document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("TrdSel")& "_AlpTrendGrp").value=me.value
	end if</SCRIPT>

me.style.pixelleft=me.style.pixelleft-336
shape001_TrdTbl.style.visibility="hidden"
shape001_RecProtected.style.pixelwidth = 0
window.settimeout "upd_chart", 1000
window.settimeout "is_empty", 200</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript id=773290FF-6AE3-4D5E-81BC-29B0D684F185 defer 
shaperefcount="1">'This shape provides the ability to view, edit and invoke trend groups.
dim tid				'Timer ID
dim bGroupSelected	'Used if All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is missing to ensure the operator responce
dim sParameter		'Used to store the parameter to be added in the selected trend group
dim bLoaded			'Used to to ensure once off update of the chart shape if present 
dim oWindow_Tnd			'Used to reload trend in SafeView Environment when trace added
dim selrecoffset	'Used to reload trend in SafeView Environment when trace added
dim bTrendSet		'Used to set the trend number to the last selected trend

'Check the number of empty pens left in the currently selected trend group. Called within AlpTrendGrp.onupdate
sub is_empty
	dim tagn, e_pl, pointpar, oShape, oShape_sel
	on error resume next
	if isnull(page.getattribute("TrdSel")) then exit sub
	set oshape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	set oshape_sel=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("trdsel"))
	if oShape.objects("alpisprotected").value = 1 and window.external.securitylevel<4 then 
   		oShape_sel.title = "Protected"
		if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Protected"
		if oshape.objects("TrdTbl").style.visibility = "visible" then oshape.objects("RecProtected").style.pixelwidth=400
		exit sub		 
	else
		oshape.objects("RecProtected").style.pixelwidth=0
	end if	
	e_pl =0
	for i= 0 to 31   
		pointpar = oShape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		if pointpar=" " then e_pl=e_pl+1
	next
	oShape_sel.title = "Free Pens " & e_pl
	if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Free Pens " & e_pl
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "is_empty ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Update the contents of the chart shape object with the pens in the currently selected trend group. Called within AlpTrendGrp.onupdate
sub upd_chart
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	if isnull(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH")) then exit sub
	if isnull(page.getattribute("TrdSel")) then exit sub
	set oshape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	for each oObj in page.all.tags("DIV")
  		if oObj.classname="hsc.shape.1" then
     		if not (oObj.objects("Custtrd") is nothing) then
				for i=0 to 31
	      			point = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		  			par = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").getvalue(i)		  							
					if oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)<>"" then oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)=" "					
					oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.parameter(i+1)=""
					if asc(left(point,1))<>32 then
		     			oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)=point
		     			oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.parameter(i+1)=par
						oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PlotVisible(i+1)=true
		  			end if
				next    
     		end if
  		end if 
	next
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "upd_chart ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Prompt to select a trend group in case All_TrdSel.sha is not present in the display
sub trdresp
	on error resume next
	if typename(window.external)<>"IHDXPopupWindow2" then
		set	oPage=page
		set oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oPage=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.document.all.page 
		set oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if
	
	if bCancel=true then 
    	window.clearinterval(tid)
		bCancel=false
		bTrdResp=false
		bGroupSelected = false
    	exit sub
	end if
	istatus=oWnd.getresponse3 ( "No trend group selected! Please select one. ", 0, resp)
	if istatus=1 and resp<>"" then 
		trdg = csng(resp)
		if err.number = 0 and trdg > 0 then 	
			set oshape_TrendSup=oPage.document.getelementbyid(oPage.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
			oshape_TrendSup.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value = trdg
		else
    		oWnd.messagezonetext "this is not a valid trend number!"
			istatus=0
		end if
		bTrdResp=false
		bGroupSelected = true
    	window.clearinterval (tid)
		call add2trend(sParameter)
	end if
	if istatus=0 then window.clearinterval (tid)
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "trdresp ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'Add the parameter stored in sParameter to the selected trend group
sub add2trend(sparam)
	dim oshape, oshape_c, oshape_sel,cm_point, cm_param, trd_num, empty_place, pointpar, tagn
	on error resume next
	if typename(window.external)<>"IHDXPopupWindow2" then
		set	oPage=page
		set oWnd=window.external
    else
	    set	oPage=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.document.all.page 
		set oWnd=window.external.parentdocument.parentwindow.external
	end if

	set oshape_sel=oPage.document.getelementbyid(oPage.getattribute("trdsel"))
	selrecoffset=oshape_sel.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value
	set oshape = page.document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	set oshape_c=oPage.document.getelementbyid(oPage.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	Trd_Par= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParam)
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_Par,".")
	sPoint_CP= Mid(Trd_Par,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameter_CP = Mid(Trd_Par, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_point = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPoint_CP)
	if cm_point = "" then cm_point = sPoint_cp
	if instr(cm_point,"{%")>0 then cm_point=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
	cm_param = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameter_CP)
	if cm_param = "" then cm_param = sParameter_CP
	trd_num = oShape_c.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value
	if isnull(oPage.getattribute("TrdSel")) and bGroupSelected = false then 
		sParameter = sparam 
		oPage.focus
		bTrdResp=true
		tid = window.setinterval ("trdresp",500)
    	exit sub
	end if
	if oShape_c.objects("alpisprotected").value = 1 and oWnd.securitylevel<4 then 
		oWnd.messagezonetext "The trend group no " & trd_num & " is protected!"
		exit sub
	end if	 
	exist = 0
	empty_place = -1
	for i=0 to 31
		pointpar = oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		if ucase(pointpar) = ucase (cm_point) and ucase(oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").getvalue(i)) = ucase (cm_param) then exist = 1	
		if pointpar=" " and empty_place = -1 then empty_place = i 
	next
	if exist = 0 then
		if empty_place >= 0 then
			oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").setvalue empty_place, cm_point
			oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").setvalue empty_place, cm_param
			free = right(oShape_sel.title,2)
			free=free-1
        	oShape_sel.title="Free Pens " & free
			if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Free Pens " & free
			oWnd.messagezonetext ucase(cm_point) & "." & ucase(cm_param) & " Has been added to trend group no " & trd_num
			set oSview=getobject("","Honeywell.Workspace.Client")
			If oSview.IsManagerActive = true then
				reload_trend=false
				reload_popup=false
				for each oWndw in ownd.parent.StationWindows
					recoffset=oWndw.currentrecordoffset
					currpage=oWndw.currentpage 
					if lcase(currpage)="systnd03.htm" and cint(recoffset)=cint(selrecoffset) then                      
					   reload_trend=true
					   set oWindow_Tnd=oWndw										   
					elseif lcase(currpage)="chart_popup_cp.htm" then
						if cint(selrecoffset)=cint(mid(oWndw.document.title,14)) then 
					   		reload_popup=true
					   		set oWindow_Pop=oWndw					
						end if
					end if
				next
				if reload_trend=true then 
					oSview.setoutputfocuswnd(oWindow_Tnd.AppWindow.HWND)
					window.setTimeout "reloadTrend", 1000 
				end if	
				if reload_popup=true then 
					oSview.setoutputfocuswnd(oWindow_Pop.AppWindow.HWND)
					window.setTimeout "reloadPopup", 1000 
				end if
			else
				set chrt_pop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("dummy" & selrecoffset)
				if typename(chrt_pop)<>"Nothing" then
					chrt_pop.close
					window.setTimeout "reloadPopup", 1000
				end if
			end if	
		else 
			oWnd.messagezonetext "Trend group no " & trd_num & " is full!"
		end if
	else
		oWnd.messagezonetext ucase(cm_point) & "." & ucase(cm_param) & " Has been already configured in trend group no " & trd_num 
	end if
	window.settimeout "upd_chart", 1000
	bGroupSelected = false
	if err.number <> 0 then oWnd.LogMessage "add2trend ->" & oShape.id & " " & sparam & " " & err.description
end sub

sub reloadTrend()
	oWindow_Tnd.requestserverlrn 21, 8, selrecoffset
end sub

sub reloadPopup()
	call trd_popup
end sub</SCRIPT>

<SCRIPT language=VBScript id=89C3751B-05B4-4247-9C2F-BC2E1A7FB362 defer 
shaperefcount="1">sub PbNav_Alarm_Indication(oSource)
	dim oShape,iUnack,iAck
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if instr(oSource.id,"Right")>0 then sDir=oShape.getcustomProperty("text","cp_right")
	if instr(oSource.id,"Left")>0 then sDir=oShape.getcustomProperty("text","cp_left")
	if instr(oSource.id,"Down")>0 then sDir=oShape.getcustomProperty("text","cp_down")
	if instr(oSource.id,"UP")>0 then sDir=oShape.getcustomProperty("text","cp_up")
    sParam=sDir & "_G.name"	
    iPr=(oSource.dataalarm(sparam) and 48)/16
	iUnack=(oSource.dataalarm(sparam) and 1024)=1024
	if iUnack = True then
	  	oSource.fillcolorblink=true
	else
   		oSource.fillcolorblink=false
	end if
	
	select case iPr
    case 0
	    oSource.styleclass="Buttons"			
	case 1
		oSource.fillcolor=AlarmStateIconfactory.lowColor	
	case 2
		oSource.fillcolor=AlarmStateIconfactory.highColor
	case 3
		oSource.fillcolor=AlarmStateIconfactory.urgentColor    
	end select
end sub</SCRIPT>
